Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any homological module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 872. The projective resolution has length at most 364 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_872(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 872. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^364(S, F) is trivial. QED.
